@bp.route('/stages/<stage_id>/matches', methods=['POST'])
@jwt_required()
@admin_required
def create_match(stage_id):
    stage = Stage.query.get_or_404(stage_id)
    data = request.get_json()
    home_team = Team.query.get(data['home_team_id'])
    away_team = Team.query.get(data['away_team_id'])
    if not home_team or not away_team:
        abort(400, "Team not found")

    match = CompetitionMatch(
        season_id=stage.season_id,
        stage_id=stage.id,
        group_id=data.get('group_id'),
        home_team_id=data['home_team_id'],
        away_team_id=data['away_team_id'],
        match_date=datetime.fromisoformat(data['match_date']),
        venue=data.get('venue'),
        is_ticketed=data.get('is_ticketed', False)
    )
    db.session.add(match)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify(match.to_dict()), 201

@bp.route('/matches/<match_id>', methods=['PUT'])
@jwt_required()
@admin_required
def update_match(match_id):
    match = CompetitionMatch.query.get_or_404(match_id)
    data = request.get_json()
    for field in ['match_date', 'venue', 'home_score', 'away_score', 'status', 'is_ticketed']:
        if field in data:
            if field == 'match_date':
                match.match_date = datetime.fromisoformat(data[field])
            else:
                setattr(match, field, data[field])

    if data.get('status') == 'finished' and match.status != 'finished':
        db.session.commit()
        if match.group_id:
            recalc_standings(group_id=match.group_id)
        else:
            recalc_standings(season_id=match.season_id)
    else:
        db.session.commit()

    return jsonify(match.to_dict()), 200

@bp.route('/matches/<match_id>', methods=['DELETE'])
@jwt_required()
@admin_required
def delete_match(match_id):
    match = CompetitionMatch.query.get_or_404(match_id)
    db.session.delete(match)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({'message': 'Match deleted'}), 200

    stage = Stage.query.get_or_404(stage_id)

    return jsonify([m.to_dict() for m in matches]), 200

# ==================== STANDINGS ====================
@bp.route('/groups/<group_id>/standings', methods=['GET'])
def get_group_standings(group_id):
    group = Group.query.get_or_404(group_id)
    standings = group.standings.order_by(Standing.points.desc(), (Standing.goals_for - Standing.goals_against).desc(), Standing.goals_for.desc()).all()
    return jsonify([s.to_dict() for s in standings]), 200

@bp.route('/seasons/<season_id>/standings', methods=['GET'])
def get_season_standings(season_id):
    season = CompetitionSeason.query.get_or_404(season_id)
    # Check if any stage has groups
    groups = Group.query.join(Stage).filter(Stage.season_id == season_id).all()
    if groups:
        result = {}
        for g in groups:
            standings = Standing.query.filter_by(group_id=g.id)\
                .order_by(Standing.points.desc(), (Standing.goals_for - Standing.goals_against).desc(), Standing.goals_for.desc()).all()
            result[g.name] = [s.to_dict() for s in standings]
        return jsonify(result), 200
    else:
        # No groups, return single table
        standings = Standing.query.filter_by(season_id=season_id)\
            .order_by(Standing.points.desc(), (Standing.goals_for - Standing.goals_against).desc(), Standing.goals_for.desc()).all()
        return jsonify([s.to_dict() for s in standings]), 200

# ==================== FRIENDLIES ====================
@bp.route('/friendlies', methods=['POST'])
@jwt_required()
@admin_required
def create_friendly():
    data = request.get_json()
    home_team = Team.query.get(data['home_team_id'])
    away_team = Team.query.get(data['away_team_id'])
    if not home_team or not away_team:
        abort(400, "Team not found")

    competition = None
    if data.get('series_name'):
        series_name = data['series_name']
        competition = Competition.query.filter_by(name=series_name, competition_type='friendly_series').first()
        if not competition:
            competition = Competition(
                name=series_name,
                competition_type='friendly_series',
                gender=data.get('gender', 'mixed'),
                age_group=data.get('age_group'),
                scope='international',
                is_active=True
            )
            db.session.add(competition)
            db.session.flush()

    season = None
    if competition:
        season_name = data.get('season_name', f"Friendly Series {datetime.now().year}")
        season = CompetitionSeason.query.filter_by(competition_id=competition.id, name=season_name).first()
        if not season:
            season = CompetitionSeason(
                competition_id=competition.id,
                name=season_name,
                start_date=datetime.now().date(),
                status='active',
                is_current=True
            )
            db.session.add(season)
            db.session.flush()

    stage = None
    if season:
        stage = Stage.query.filter_by(season_id=season.id, name='Friendlies').first()
        if not stage:
            stage = Stage(
                season_id=season.id,
                name='Friendlies',
                stage_order=0,
                is_knockout=False,
                is_group_stage=False
            )
            db.session.add(stage)
            db.session.flush()

    match = CompetitionMatch(
        season_id=season.id if season else None,
        stage_id=stage.id if stage else None,
        home_team_id=data['home_team_id'],
        away_team_id=data['away_team_id'],
        match_date=datetime.fromisoformat(data['match_date']),
        venue=data.get('venue'),
        is_ticketed=data.get('is_ticketed', False),
        status='scheduled'
    )
    db.session.add(match)
    db.session.commit()

    return jsonify(match.to_dict()), 201

@bp.route('/friendlies', methods=['GET'])
def get_friendlies():
    matches = CompetitionMatch.query.join(Stage).join(CompetitionSeason).join(Competition).filter(
        Competition.competition_type == 'friendly_series'
    ).order_by(CompetitionMatch.match_date.desc()).all()
    return jsonify([m.to_dict() for m in matches]), 200

# backend/app/routes/competitions.py
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, joinedload
from typing import List, Optional
from uuid import UUID
from .. import database, models, schemas, auth

router = APIRouter()

@router.get("/")
async def get_competitions(
    gender: Optional[str] = None,
    season: Optional[str] = None,
    competition_type: Optional[str] = None,
    include_archived: bool = False,
    db: Session = Depends(database.get_db)
):
    """Get all competitions with optional filters"""
    query = db.query(models.Competition).options(
        joinedload(models.Competition.current_champion),
        joinedload(models.Competition.seasons)
    )
    
    if gender:
        query = query.filter(models.Competition.gender == gender)
    if competition_type:
        query = query.filter(models.Competition.competition_type == competition_type)
    if season:
        query = query.filter(models.Competition.season == season)
    if not include_archived:
        query = query.filter(models.Competition.is_archived == False)
    
    return query.all()

@router.get("/seasons")
async def get_available_seasons(db: Session = Depends(database.get_db)):
    """Get all unique seasons from competitions"""
    seasons = db.query(models.Competition.season).distinct().order_by(models.Competition.season.desc()).all()
    return [s[0] for s in seasons]

@router.get("/{competition_id}")
async def get_competition_detail(
    competition_id: str,
    season: Optional[str] = None,
    db: Session = Depends(database.get_db)
):
    """Get detailed competition info including standings for a specific season"""
    try:
        comp_uuid = UUID(competition_id)
    except ValueError:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid competition ID format")
    
    competition = db.query(models.Competition).filter(
        models.Competition.id == comp_uuid
    ).first()
    
    if not competition:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Competition not found")
    
    # Get standings for the specified season
    standings = []
    if season:
        # Find the league associated with this competition
        league = db.query(models.League).filter(
            models.League.name == competition.name,
            models.League.season == season
        ).first()
        
        if league:
            standings = db.query(models.LeagueStanding).options(
                joinedload(models.LeagueStanding.team)
            ).filter(
                models.LeagueStanding.league_id == league.id,
                models.LeagueStanding.season == season
            ).order_by(
                models.LeagueStanding.points.desc(),
                (models.LeagueStanding.goals_for - models.LeagueStanding.goals_against).desc(),
                models.LeagueStanding.goals_for.desc()
            ).all()
    
    # Get historical champions
    seasons_history = db.query(models.CompetitionSeason).filter(
        models.CompetitionSeason.competition_id == comp_uuid
    ).order_by(models.CompetitionSeason.season.desc()).all()
    
    return {
        "competition": competition,
        "standings": standings,
        "season": season,
        "history": seasons_history
    }

@router.get("/{competition_id}/history")
async def get_competition_history(
    competition_id: str,
    limit: int = 10,
    db: Session = Depends(database.get_db)
):
    """Get historical champions and runners-up for a competition"""
    try:
        comp_uuid = UUID(competition_id)
    except ValueError:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid competition ID format")
    
    history = db.query(models.CompetitionSeason).options(
        joinedload(models.CompetitionSeason.champion),
        joinedload(models.CompetitionSeason.runner_up)
    ).filter(
        models.CompetitionSeason.competition_id == comp_uuid
    ).order_by(
        models.CompetitionSeason.season.desc()
    ).limit(limit).all()
    
    return history

@router.post("/", response_model=schemas.CompetitionOut)
async def create_competition(
    competition: schemas.CompetitionCreate,
    current_user: models.User = Depends(auth.get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(database.get_db)
):
    """Create a new competition (admin only)"""
    if current_user.role != models.UserRole.ADMIN:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Admin only")
    
    db_competition = models.Competition(**competition.dict())
    db.add(db_competition)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(db_competition)
    return db_competition

from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, abort
from flask_jwt_extended import jwt_required
from app.database import db
from app.models import CupCompetition, CupSeason, CupMatch, Team, ContinentalQualification, League, LeagueStanding, Gender
from app.schemas import CupMatchCreate
from app.auth import get_current_user, admin_required
from datetime import datetime

bp = Blueprint('cups', __name__)

@bp.route('/', methods=['GET'])
def get_cup_competitions():
    gender = request.args.get('gender')
    season = request.args.get('season')
    query = CupCompetition.query
    if gender:
        query = query.filter_by(gender=gender)
    if season:
        query = query.filter_by(season=season)
    return jsonify([c.to_dict() for c in query.all()])

@bp.route('/<cup_id>', methods=['GET'])
def get_cup_competition(cup_id):
    cup = CupCompetition.query.get(cup_id)
    if not cup:
        abort(404)
    return jsonify(cup.to_dict())

@bp.route('/<cup_id>/seasons', methods=['GET'])
def get_cup_seasons(cup_id):
    seasons = CupSeason.query.filter_by(cup_competition_id=cup_id).order_by(CupSeason.season.desc()).all()
    return jsonify([s.to_dict() for s in seasons])

@bp.route('/<cup_id>/seasons', methods=['POST'])
@jwt_required()
def create_cup_season(cup_id):
    user = get_current_user()
    if user.role not in ['admin', 'match_commissioner']:
        abort(403)
    cup = CupCompetition.query.get(cup_id)
    if not cup:
        abort(404)
    data = request.get_json()
    season = data.get('season')
    started_at = data.get('started_at')
    if not season or not started_at:
        abort(400, "season and started_at required")
    existing = CupSeason.query.filter_by(cup_competition_id=cup_id, season=season).first()
    if existing:
        abort(400, "Season already exists")
    try:
        start_date = datetime.strptime(started_at, '%Y-%m-%d').date()
    except:
        abort(400, "Invalid date format, use YYYY-MM-DD")
    cup_season = CupSeason(
        cup_competition_id=cup_id,
        season=season,
        started_at=start_date
    )
    db.session.add(cup_season)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({"id": str(cup_season.id)}), 201

@bp.route('/seasons/<season_id>/matches', methods=['GET'])
def get_cup_matches(season_id):
    round_name = request.args.get('round_name')
    query = CupMatch.query.filter_by(cup_season_id=season_id)
    if round_name:
        query = query.filter_by(round_name=round_name)
    matches = query.order_by(CupMatch.match_date).all()
    return jsonify([m.to_dict() for m in matches])

@bp.route('/seasons/<season_id>/matches', methods=['POST'])
@jwt_required()
def add_cup_match(season_id):
    user = get_current_user()
    if user.role not in ['admin', 'match_commissioner']:
        abort(403)
    season = CupSeason.query.get(season_id)
    if not season:
        abort(404)
    data = request.get_json()
    # validate teams
    home = Team.query.get(data['home_team_id'])
    away = Team.query.get(data['away_team_id'])
    if not home or not away:
        abort(404, "One or both teams not found")
    match = CupMatch(
        cup_season_id=season_id,
        round_name=data['round_name'],
        home_team_id=data['home_team_id'],
        away_team_id=data['away_team_id'],
        match_date=datetime.fromisoformat(data['match_date']),
        venue=data['venue'],
        is_played=False
    )
    db.session.add(match)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({"id": str(match.id)}), 201

@bp.route('/matches/<match_id>', methods=['PATCH'])
@jwt_required()
def update_cup_match_result(match_id):
    user = get_current_user()
    if user.role not in ['admin', 'match_commissioner']:
        abort(403)
    match = CupMatch.query.get(match_id)
    if not match:
        abort(404)
    data = request.get_json()
    match.home_score = data.get('home_score', match.home_score)
    match.away_score = data.get('away_score', match.away_score)
    match.is_played = True
    if data.get('winner_id'):
        winner = Team.query.get(data['winner_id'])
        if not winner:
            abort(404, "Winner team not found")
        match.winner_id = data['winner_id']
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({"message": "Updated"})

# Continental Qualifications
@bp.route('/continental-qualifications', methods=['GET'])
def get_continental_qualifications():
    season = request.args.get('season')
    if not season:
        abort(400, "season required")
    quals = ContinentalQualification.query.filter_by(season=season).all()
    return jsonify([q.to_dict() for q in quals])

@bp.route('/continental-qualifications/apply', methods=['POST'])
@jwt_required()
@admin_required
def apply_continental_qualifications():
    data = request.get_json()
    season = data.get('season')
    if not season:
        abort(400, "season required")
    results = []
    # Men's Super League winner and runner-up
    men_super = League.query.filter_by(name="MTN Super League", season=season).first()
    if men_super:
        standings = LeagueStanding.query.filter_by(league_id=men_super.id, season=season)\
            .order_by(LeagueStanding.points.desc()).limit(2).all()
        if len(standings) >= 1:
            champ = standings[0]
            # CAF Champions League
            existing = ContinentalQualification.query.filter_by(season=season, competition_name="CAF Champions League", qualifying_team_id=champ.team_id).first()
            if not existing:
                qual = ContinentalQualification(
                    season=season,
                    competition_name="CAF Champions League",
                    gender=Gender.MALE.value,
                    qualifying_team_id=champ.team_id,
                    qualification_reason="Super League Winner"
                )
                db.session.add(qual)
                results.append({"team": champ.team.name, "competition": "CAF Champions League"})
        if len(standings) >= 2:
            runner = standings[1]
            existing = ContinentalQualification.query.filter_by(season=season, competition_name="CAF Confederation Cup", qualifying_team_id=runner.team_id).first()
            if not existing:
                qual = ContinentalQualification(
                    season=season,
                    competition_name="CAF Confederation Cup",
                    gender=Gender.MALE.value,
                    qualifying_team_id=runner.team_id,
                    qualification_reason="Super League Runner-up"
                )
                db.session.add(qual)
                results.append({"team": runner.team.name, "competition": "CAF Confederation Cup"})
    # Women's Super League winner -> COSAFA Women's Champions League
    women_super = League.query.filter_by(name="Women's Super League", season=season).first()
    if women_super:
        winner = LeagueStanding.query.filter_by(league_id=women_super.id, season=season)\
            .order_by(LeagueStanding.points.desc()).first()
        if winner:
            existing = ContinentalQualification.query.filter_by(season=season, competition_name="COSAFA Women's Champions League", qualifying_team_id=winner.team_id).first()
            if not existing:
                qual = ContinentalQualification(
                    season=season,
                    competition_name="COSAFA Women's Champions League",
                    gender=Gender.FEMALE.value,
                    qualifying_team_id=winner.team_id,
                    qualification_reason="Women's Super League Winner"
                )
                db.session.add(qual)
                results.append({"team": winner.team.name, "competition": "COSAFA Women's Champions League"})
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({"message": f"Continental qualifications applied for {season}", "qualifications": results})


====================================================================================================
FILE: app\routes\leagues.py

from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, abort
from flask_jwt_extended import jwt_required, get_jwt_identity
from app.database import db
from app.models import League, LeagueStanding, PromotionRelegationRule, PromotionRelegation
from app.schemas import LeagueCreate
from app.auth import get_current_user, admin_required
import uuid

bp = Blueprint('leagues', __name__)

@bp.route('/', methods=['GET'])
def get_leagues():
    gender = request.args.get('gender')
    season = request.args.get('season')
    query = League.query
    if gender:
        query = query.filter_by(gender=gender)
    if season:
        query = query.filter_by(season=season)
    return jsonify([l.to_dict() for l in query.all()])

@bp.route('/<league_id>', methods=['GET'])
def get_league(league_id):
    try:
        lid = uuid.UUID(league_id)
    except:
        abort(400)
    league = League.query.get(lid)
    if not league:
        abort(404)
    return jsonify(league.to_dict())

@bp.route('/<league_id>/standings', methods=['GET'])
def get_league_standings(league_id):
    try:
        lid = uuid.UUID(league_id)
    except:
        abort(400)
    league = League.query.get(lid)
    if not league:
        abort(404)
    season = request.args.get('season')
    if not season:
        abort(400, "season required")
    
    # Eagerly load the team relationship
    standings = LeagueStanding.query.options(
        db.joinedload(LeagueStanding.team)
    ).filter(
        LeagueStanding.league_id == lid,
        LeagueStanding.season == season
    ).order_by(
        LeagueStanding.points.desc(),
        (LeagueStanding.goals_for - LeagueStanding.goals_against).desc(),
        LeagueStanding.goals_for.desc()
    ).all()
    
    # Convert to dict with team data
    result = []
    for s in standings:
        data = s.to_dict()
        # Add team info
        if s.team:
            data['team'] = {
                'id': str(s.team.id),
                'name': s.team.name,
                'short_name': s.team.short_name,
                'logo': s.team.logo
            }
        result.append(data)
    
    return jsonify(result)

@bp.route('/promotion-relegation/apply', methods=['POST'])
@jwt_required()
@admin_required
def apply_promotion_relegation():
    data = request.get_json()
    season = data.get('season')
    if not season:
        abort(400, "season required")
    rules = PromotionRelegationRule.query.filter_by(season=season).all()
    results = []
    for rule in rules:
        standings = LeagueStanding.query.filter_by(league_id=rule.from_league_id, season=season)\
            .order_by(LeagueStanding.points.desc()).limit(rule.positions_promoted if rule.positions_promoted > 0 else rule.positions_relegated).all()
        for standing in standings:
            action = "promoted" if rule.positions_promoted > 0 else "relegated"
            team = standing.team
            target_league = League.query.get(rule.to_league_id)
            if team and target_league:
                team.current_league_level = target_league.level
                team.league_tier = target_league.tier
                pr = PromotionRelegation(
                    team_id=team.id,
                    from_league_id=rule.from_league_id,
                    to_league_id=rule.to_league_id,
                    season=season,
                    action=action
                )
                db.session.add(pr)
                results.append({
                    "team": team.name,
                    "action": action,
                    "from_league": rule.from_league.name,
                    "to_league": target_league.name
                })
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({"message": "Promotion/relegation applied", "results": results})

from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, abort
from flask_jwt_extended import jwt_required
from app.database import db
from app.models import MatchEvent, Match, Player
from app.schemas import MatchEventCreate
from app.auth import get_current_user

bp = Blueprint('match_events', __name__)

@bp.route('/', methods=['POST'])
@jwt_required()
def create_event():
    user = get_current_user()
    if user.role not in ['admin', 'match_commissioner']:
        abort(403)
    data = request.get_json()
    match = Match.query.get(data['match_id'])
    if not match:
        abort(404, "Match not found")
    player = Player.query.get(data['player_id'])
    if not player:
        abort(404, "Player not found")
    if data.get('related_player_id'):
        related = Player.query.get(data['related_player_id'])
        if not related:
            abort(404, "Related player not found")
    event = MatchEvent(
        match_id=data['match_id'],
        event_type=data['event_type'],
        player_id=data['player_id'],
        related_player_id=data.get('related_player_id'),
        minute=data['minute'],
        minute_added=data.get('minute_added', 0),
        description=data.get('description')
    )
    db.session.add(event)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({"id": str(event.id)}), 201

@bp.route('/match/<match_id>', methods=['GET'])
def get_events(match_id):
    events = MatchEvent.query.filter_by(match_id=match_id).order_by(MatchEvent.minute).all()
    return jsonify([e.to_dict() for e in events])

@jwt_required()
def delete_event(event_id):
    user = get_current_user()
    if user.role not in ['admin', 'match_commissioner']:
        abort(403)
    event = MatchEvent.query.get(event_id)
    if not event:
        abort(404)
    db.session.delete(event)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({"message": "Deleted"})

FILE: app\routes\matches.py

from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, abort, Response
from flask_jwt_extended import jwt_required
from app.database import db
from app.models import Match, Team, CompetitionMatch
from app.schemas import MatchCreate
from app.auth import get_current_user
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
import pytz
from icalendar import Calendar, Event

bp = Blueprint('matches', __name__)

@bp.route('/', methods=['GET'])
def get_matches():
    status = request.args.get('status')
    competition = request.args.get('competition')
    skip = int(request.args.get('skip', 0))
    limit = int(request.args.get('limit', 20))

    # Fetch from old Match table
    query = Match.query
    if status:
        query = query.filter_by(status=status)
    if competition:
        query = query.filter_by(competition=competition)
    if status == 'upcoming':
        query = query.filter(Match.match_date >= datetime.now())
        matches = query.order_by(Match.match_date.asc()).offset(skip).limit(limit).all()
    else:
        matches = query.order_by(Match.match_date.desc()).offset(skip).limit(limit).all()

    # Fetch from CompetitionMatch (competition management matches)
    comp_query = CompetitionMatch.query
    if status:
        if status == 'upcoming':
            comp_query = comp_query.filter(CompetitionMatch.status == 'scheduled', CompetitionMatch.match_date >= datetime.now())
        elif status == 'finished':
            comp_query = comp_query.filter(CompetitionMatch.status == 'finished')
        elif status == 'live':
            comp_query = comp_query.filter(CompetitionMatch.status == 'live')
    # Note: competition filter by name is complex; skip for simplicity
    comp_matches = comp_query.order_by(CompetitionMatch.match_date.asc()).all()

    # Combine and sort by date
    combined = list(matches) + list(comp_matches)
    combined.sort(key=lambda x: x.match_date)
    result = combined[skip:skip+limit]
    return jsonify([m.to_dict() for m in result])


@bp.route('/latest', methods=['GET'])
def get_latest_matches():
    limit = int(request.args.get('limit', 5))
    matches = Match.query.filter_by(status='finished').order_by(Match.match_date.desc()).limit(limit).all()
    return jsonify([m.to_dict() for m in matches])

@bp.route('/upcoming', methods=['GET'])
def get_upcoming_matches():
    limit = int(request.args.get('limit', 5))
    matches = Match.query.filter(Match.status == 'upcoming', Match.match_date >= datetime.now())\
        .order_by(Match.match_date.asc()).limit(limit).all()
    return jsonify([m.to_dict() for m in matches])

@bp.route('/<match_id>', methods=['GET'])
def get_match(match_id):
    match = Match.query.get(match_id)
    if not match:
        abort(404)
    return jsonify(match.to_dict())

@bp.route('/<match_id>/score', methods=['PATCH'])
@jwt_required()
def update_match_score(match_id):
    user = get_current_user()
    if user.role not in ['admin', 'match_commissioner']:
        abort(403)
    match = Match.query.get(match_id)
    if not match:
        abort(404)
    data = request.get_json()
    match.home_score = data.get('home_score', match.home_score)
    match.away_score = data.get('away_score', match.away_score)
    match.status = data.get('status', 'finished')
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({"message": "Score updated"})

@bp.route('/export/calendar', methods=['GET'])
def export_calendar():
    team_id = request.args.get('team_id')
    competition = request.args.get('competition')
    query = Match.query.filter(Match.status != 'finished')
    if team_id:
        team = Team.query.get(team_id)
        if team:
            query = query.filter((Match.home_team == team.name) | (Match.away_team == team.name))
    if competition:
        query = query.filter_by(competition=competition)
    matches = query.all()
    cal = Calendar()
    cal.add('prodid', '-//FAZ Match Calendar//faz.zm//')
    cal.add('version', '2.0')
    for match in matches:
        event = Event()
        event.add('summary', f"{match.home_team} vs {match.away_team}")
        if match.match_date.tzinfo is None:
            match_date = pytz.UTC.localize(match.match_date)
        else:
            match_date = match.match_date
        event.add('dtstart', match_date)
        event.add('dtend', match_date + timedelta(hours=2))
        event.add('location', match.venue)
        event.add('description', f"Competition: {match.competition}\nStatus: {match.status}")
        cal.add_component(event)
    headers = {'Content-Disposition': 'attachment; filename="faz_matches.ics"'}
    return Response(cal.to_ical(), mimetype='text/calendar', headers=headers)

@bp.route('/referee/<referee_id>', methods=['GET'])
def get_matches_by_referee(referee_id):
    status = request.args.get('status')
    query = Match.query.filter_by(referee_id=referee_id)
    if status:
        query = query.filter_by(status=status)
    matches = query.order_by(Match.match_date.asc()).all()
    return jsonify([m.to_dict() for m in matches])

from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, abort
from flask_jwt_extended import jwt_required
from app.database import db
from app.models import NationalTeam, NationalTeamFixture, TournamentParticipation, Tournament
from app.schemas import NationalTeamCreate, NationalTeamFixtureCreate
from app.auth import get_current_user
import uuid

bp = Blueprint('national_teams', __name__)

@bp.route('/', methods=['GET'])
def get_national_teams():
    gender = request.args.get('gender')
    age_group = request.args.get('age_group')
    query = NationalTeam.query
    if gender:
        query = query.filter_by(gender=gender)
    if age_group:
        query = query.filter_by(age_group=age_group)
    return jsonify([t.to_dict() for t in query.all()])

    if user.role not in ['admin', 'media_officer']:
        abort(403)
    try:
        tid = uuid.UUID(team_id)
    except:
        abort(400)
    team = NationalTeam.query.get(tid)
    if not team:
        abort(404)
    data = request.get_json()
    fixture = NationalTeamFixture(
        national_team_id=tid,
        opponent=data['opponent'],
        match_date=datetime.fromisoformat(data['match_date']),
        venue=data['venue'],
        competition=data['competition'],
        match_type=data['match_type'],
        home_away=data['home_away'],

    )

    if not fixture:
        abort(404)
    data = request.get_json()
    fixture.goals_for = data.get('goals_for', fixture.goals_for)
    fixture.goals_against = data.get('goals_against', fixture.goals_against)
    fixture.result = data.get('result', fixture.result)
    fixture.is_played = True
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({"message": "Updated"})

from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, abort
from app.database import db
from app.models import News
from sqlalchemy import func, desc

bp = Blueprint('news', __name__)

@bp.route('/', methods=['GET'])
def get_news():
    skip = int(request.args.get('skip', 0))
    limit = int(request.args.get('limit', 12))
    news = News.query.filter_by(is_published=True).order_by(News.published_at.desc()).offset(skip).limit(limit).all()
    return jsonify([n.to_dict() for n in news])

@bp.route('/featured', methods=['GET'])
def get_featured_news():
    limit = int(request.args.get('limit', 3))
    news = News.query.filter_by(is_published=True).order_by(News.views.desc()).limit(limit).all()
    return jsonify([n.to_dict() for n in news])

@bp.route('/search', methods=['GET'])
def search_news():
    q = request.args.get('q')
    if not q:
        return jsonify([])
    news = News.query.filter(News.is_published == True, (News.title.ilike(f"%{q}%") | News.content.ilike(f"%{q}%") | News.excerpt.ilike(f"%{q}%"))).order_by(News.published_at.desc()).all()
    return jsonify([n.to_dict() for n in news])

@bp.route('/filter', methods=['GET'])
def filter_news():
    month = request.args.get('month')
    year = request.args.get('year')
    query = News.query.filter_by(is_published=True)
    month_map = {
        "January": 1, "February": 2, "March": 3, "April": 4,
        "May": 5, "June": 6, "July": 7, "August": 8,
        "September": 9, "October": 10, "November": 11, "December": 12
    }
    if month and month in month_map:
        query = query.filter(func.extract('month', News.published_at) == month_map[month])
    if year:
        query = query.filter(func.extract('year', News.published_at) == int(year))
    news = query.order_by(News.published_at.desc()).all()
    return jsonify([n.to_dict() for n in news])

@bp.route('/years', methods=['GET'])
def get_years():
    years = db.session.query(func.distinct(func.extract('year', News.published_at))).filter(News.is_published == True).order_by(func.extract('year', News.published_at).desc()).all()
    years_list = [int(y[0]) for y in years if y[0] is not None]
    return jsonify(years_list)

@bp.route('/<news_id>', methods=['GET'])
def get_news_item(news_id):
    news = News.query.get(news_id)
    if not news:
        abort(404)
    news.views += 1
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify(news.to_dict())

FILE: app\routes\reports.py

from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, abort
from flask_jwt_extended import jwt_required
from app.database import db
from app.models import MatchReport, Match
from app.auth import get_current_user

bp = Blueprint('reports', __name__)

@bp.route('/match-reports', methods=['POST'])
@jwt_required()
def submit_report():
    user = get_current_user()
    if user.role != 'referee':
        abort(403, "Only referees can submit reports")
    data = request.get_json()
    match = Match.query.get(data['match_id'])
    if not match:
        abort(404, "Match not found")
    if match.referee_id != user.id:
        abort(403, "This match is not assigned to you")
    existing = MatchReport.query.filter_by(match_id=data['match_id']).first()
    if existing:
        abort(400, "Report already submitted")
    report = MatchReport(
        match_id=data['match_id'],
        referee_id=user.id,
        content=data['content'],
        yellow_cards=data.get('yellow_cards', []),
        red_cards=data.get('red_cards', []),
        incidents=data.get('incidents', [])
    )
    db.session.add(report)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({"id": str(report.id)}), 201

@bp.route('/match-reports/match/<match_id>', methods=['GET'])
def get_report_by_match(match_id):
    report = MatchReport.query.filter_by(match_id=match_id).first()
    if not report:
        abort(404)
    return jsonify(report.to_dict())

@bp.route('/match-reports/<report_id>', methods=['PUT'])
@jwt_required()
def update_report(report_id):
    user = get_current_user()
    report = MatchReport.query.get(report_id)
    if not report:
        abort(404)
    if report.referee_id != user.id:
        abort(403)
    data = request.get_json()
    report.content = data.get('content', report.content)
    report.yellow_cards = data.get('yellow_cards', report.yellow_cards)
    report.red_cards = data.get('red_cards', report.red_cards)
    report.incidents = data.get('incidents', report.incidents)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({"message": "Report updated"})

from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, abort, make_response
from flask_jwt_extended import jwt_required, get_jwt_identity, verify_jwt_in_request
from app.database import db
from app.models import ProductCategory, Product, Cart, CartItem, Order, OrderItem
from app.schemas import ProductCategoryCreate, ProductCreate, AddToCart, CheckoutData, PaymentInitiate
from app.auth import get_current_user, get_current_user_optional, admin_required
import uuid, os, requests, re
from datetime import datetime

bp = Blueprint('shop', __name__)

PAWAPAY_API_KEY = os.environ.get('PAWAPAY_API_KEY')
PAWAPAY_BASE_URL = "https://api.pawapay.io/v2"

def get_pawapay_headers():
    return {
        "Authorization": f"Bearer {PAWAPAY_API_KEY}",
        "Content-Type": "application/json"
    }

@bp.route('/products', methods=['GET'])
def get_products():
    category_id = request.args.get('category_id')
    featured = request.args.get('featured') == 'true'
    skip = int(request.args.get('skip', 0))
    limit = int(request.args.get('limit', 20))
    query = Product.query.filter_by(is_active=True)
    if category_id:
        query = query.filter_by(category_id=category_id)
    if featured:
        query = query.filter_by(is_featured=True)
    products = query.order_by(Product.created_at.desc()).offset(skip).limit(limit).all()
    return jsonify([p.to_dict() for p in products])

# ============ CART ============
@bp.route('/cart', methods=['GET'])
def get_cart():
    session_id = request.cookies.get('cart_session_id')
    auth_header = request.headers.get('Authorization')
    user = None
    if auth_header and auth_header.startswith('Bearer '):
        token = auth_header.split(' ')[1]
        # try to get user
        try:
            verify_jwt_in_request()
            user = get_current_user()  # but we need optional
        except:
            pass
    # For optional user, we can use a helper
    if user:
        cart = Cart.query.filter_by(user_id=user.id).first()
    else:
        cart = Cart.query.filter_by(session_id=session_id).first() if session_id else None
    if not cart:
        return jsonify({"items": [], "subtotal": 0, "total": 0})
    items = []
    subtotal = 0
    for item in cart.items:
        product = item.product
        if product:
            item_data = {
                "id": str(item.id),
                "product_id": str(product.id),
                "product_name": product.name,
                "product_image": product.image_url,
                "quantity": item.quantity,
                "size": item.size,
                "color": item.color,
                "unit_price": item.price_at_add,
                "subtotal": item.quantity * item.price_at_add
            }
            items.append(item_data)
            subtotal += item.quantity * item.price_at_add
    return jsonify({"items": items, "subtotal": subtotal, "total": subtotal})

@bp.route('/cart/add', methods=['POST'])
def add_to_cart():
    data = request.get_json()
    # get session_id
    session_id = request.cookies.get('cart_session_id')
    if not session_id:
        session_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    # check user
    auth_header = request.headers.get('Authorization')
    user = None
    if auth_header and auth_header.startswith('Bearer '):
        token = auth_header.split(' ')[1]
        try:
            verify_jwt_in_request()
            user = get_current_user()
        except:
            pass
    # get or create cart
    cart = None
    if user:
        cart = Cart.query.filter_by(user_id=user.id).first()
    if not cart and session_id:
        cart = Cart.query.filter_by(session_id=session_id).first()
    if not cart:
        cart = Cart(
            user_id=user.id if user else None,
            session_id=session_id
        )
        db.session.add(cart)
        db.session.commit()
    # get product
    product = Product.query.get(data['product_id'])
    if not product:
        abort(404, "Product not found")
    if product.stock_quantity < data.get('quantity', 1):
        abort(400, "Insufficient stock")
    # check existing item
    existing_item = CartItem.query.filter_by(cart_id=cart.id, product_id=product.id, size=data.get('size'), color=data.get('color')).first()
    if existing_item:
        existing_item.quantity += data.get('quantity', 1)
    else:
        item = CartItem(
            cart_id=cart.id,
            product_id=product.id,
            quantity=data.get('quantity', 1),
            size=data.get('size'),
            color=data.get('color'),
            price_at_add=product.price
        )
        db.session.add(item)
    db.session.commit()
    response = jsonify({"message": "Item added to cart", "session_id": session_id})
    response.set_cookie('cart_session_id', session_id, max_age=2592000, path='/', httponly=True, samesite='Lax')
    return response

@bp.route('/cart/update/<item_id>', methods=['PUT'])
def update_cart_item(item_id):
    data = request.get_json()
    quantity = data.get('quantity')
    item = CartItem.query.get(item_id)
    if not item:
        abort(404)
    if quantity <= 0:
        db.session.delete(item)
    else:
        item.quantity = quantity
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({"message": "Cart updated"})

@bp.route('/cart/remove/<item_id>', methods=['DELETE'])
def remove_cart_item(item_id):
    item = CartItem.query.get(item_id)
    if not item:
        abort(404)
    db.session.delete(item)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({"message": "Item removed"})

# ============ CHECKOUT ============
@bp.route('/checkout', methods=['POST'])
def checkout():
    data = request.get_json()
    session_id = request.cookies.get('cart_session_id')
    auth_header = request.headers.get('Authorization')
    user = None
    if auth_header and auth_header.startswith('Bearer '):
        token = auth_header.split(' ')[1]
        try:
            verify_jwt_in_request()
            user = get_current_user()
        except:
            pass
    cart = None
    if user:
        cart = Cart.query.filter_by(user_id=user.id).first()
    if not cart and session_id:
        cart = Cart.query.filter_by(session_id=session_id).first()
    if not cart or not cart.items:
        abort(400, "Cart is empty")
    # validate required fields
    required = ['customer_name', 'customer_email', 'customer_phone', 'shipping_address']
    for field in required:
        if not data.get(field):
            abort(400, f"{field} is required")
    subtotal = sum(item.quantity * item.price_at_add for item in cart.items)
    order_number = f"FAZ{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d')}{str(uuid.uuid4())[:8].upper()}"
    order = Order(
        order_number=order_number,
        user_id=user.id if user else None,
        customer_name=data['customer_name'],
        customer_email=data['customer_email'],
        customer_phone=data['customer_phone'],
        shipping_address=data['shipping_address'],
        subtotal=subtotal,
        delivery_fee=0,
        total_amount=subtotal,
        payment_status='pending',
        order_status='pending'
    )
    db.session.add(order)
    db.session.flush()
    for cart_item in cart.items:
        product = cart_item.product
        if product:
            order_item = OrderItem(
                order_id=order.id,
                product_id=product.id,
                product_name=product.name,
                product_image=product.image_url,
                quantity=cart_item.quantity,
                size=cart_item.size,
                color=cart_item.color,
                unit_price=cart_item.price_at_add,
                subtotal=cart_item.quantity * cart_item.price_at_add
            )
            db.session.add(order_item)
    # clear cart
    for item in cart.items:
        db.session.delete(item)
    db.session.delete(cart)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({"order_id": str(order.id), "order_number": order_number, "total_amount": order.total_amount})

# ============ PAYMENT ============
@bp.route('/payment/initiate', methods=['POST'])
def initiate_payment():
    data = request.get_json()
    order_id = data.get('order_id')
    provider = data.get('provider')
    phone_number = data.get('phone_number')
    if not all([order_id, provider, phone_number]):
        abort(400, "Missing required fields")
    order = Order.query.get(order_id)
    if not order:
        abort(404, "Order not found")
    if order.payment_status != 'pending':
        abort(400, f"Order already processed. Status: {order.payment_status}")
    # format phone
    raw_phone = phone_number
    clean_digits = re.sub(r'\D', '', raw_phone)
    if clean_digits.startswith('0'):
        clean_digits = clean_digits[1:]
    if clean_digits.startswith('260'):
        clean_digits = clean_digits[3:]
    if clean_digits.startswith('26'):
        clean_digits = clean_digits[2:]
    if len(clean_digits) != 9:
        abort(400, "Invalid phone number. Expected 9 digits after cleaning.")
    formatted_phone = f"260{clean_digits}"
    deposit_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    client_ref = f"FAZ{order.order_number[:20]}".replace('-', '').replace('_', '')
    payload = {
        "depositId": deposit_id,
        "payer": {
            "type": "MMO",
            "accountDetails": {
                "phoneNumber": formatted_phone,
                "provider": provider
            }
        },
        "amount": str(order.total_amount),
        "currency": "ZMW",
        "clientReferenceId": client_ref,
        "customerMessage": "FAZ Payment",
        "metadata": [
            {"orderId": str(order.id)},
            {"customerId": order.customer_email, "isPII": True}
        ]
    }
    try:
        resp = requests.post(f"{PAWAPAY_BASE_URL}/deposits", json=payload, headers=get_pawapay_headers(), timeout=30)
        pawa_resp = resp.json()
        if resp.status_code == 200:
            status = pawa_resp.get('status')
            if status in ['ACCEPTED', 'DUPLICATE_IGNORED']:
                order.payment_reference = pawa_resp.get('depositId', deposit_id)
                order.payment_method = provider
                db.session.commit()
                return jsonify({'success': True, 'depositId': order.payment_reference, 'message': 'Payment request sent.'})
            else:
                order.payment_status = 'failed'
                db.session.commit()
                return jsonify({'success': False, 'status': status, 'failureReason': pawa_resp.get('failureReason')})
        else:
            order.payment_status = 'failed'
            db.session.commit()
            return jsonify({'success': False, 'status': 'ERROR'})
    except Exception as e:
        abort(503, f"Payment service unavailable: {str(e)}")

@bp.route('/payment/status/<deposit_id>', methods=['GET'])
def check_payment_status(deposit_id):
    order = Order.query.filter_by(payment_reference=deposit_id).first()
    if not order:
        abort(404, "Deposit not found")
    try:
        resp = requests.get(f"{PAWAPAY_BASE_URL}/deposits/{deposit_id}", headers=get_pawapay_headers(), timeout=10)
        data = resp.json()
        if resp.status_code == 200 and data.get('status') == 'FOUND':
            deposit_data = data.get('data')
            deposit_status = deposit_data.get('status')
            if deposit_status == 'COMPLETED':
                for item in order.items:
                    product = Product.query.get(item.product_id)
                    if product:
                        product.stock_quantity -= item.quantity
                order.payment_status = 'paid'
                order.order_status = 'processing'
                db.session.commit()
                return jsonify({'success': True, 'status': 'COMPLETED'})
            elif deposit_status == 'FAILED':
                order.payment_status = 'failed'
                db.session.commit()
                return jsonify({'success': False, 'status': 'FAILED', 'failureReason': deposit_data.get('failureReason')})
            else:
                return jsonify({'success': True, 'status': deposit_status})
        else:
            order.payment_status = 'failed'
            db.session.commit()
            return jsonify({'success': False, 'status': 'ERROR'})
    except:
        abort(503, "Payment service unavailable")

@bp.route('/payment/providers', methods=['GET'])
def get_payment_providers():
    try:
        resp = requests.get(f"{PAWAPAY_BASE_URL}/availability", headers=get_pawapay_headers(), timeout=10)
        if resp.status_code == 200:
            data = resp.json()
            providers = []
            for country in data:
                if country.get('country') == 'ZMB':
                    for prov in country.get('providers', []):
                        deposit_status = prov.get('operationTypes', {}).get('DEPOSIT', 'CLOSED')
                        providers.append({
                            'provider': prov['provider'],
                            'name': prov['provider'].replace('_ZMB', '').replace('_OAPI_ZMB', '').replace('MOMO', ' Mobile Money'),
                            'country': 'ZMB',
                            'deposit_operational': deposit_status == 'OPERATIONAL'
                        })
            return jsonify(providers)
        else:
            return jsonify([])
    except:
        return jsonify([])

@bp.route('/orders/<order_id>', methods=['GET'])
def get_order(order_id):
    order = Order.query.get(order_id)
    if not order:
        abort(404)
    return jsonify(order.to_dict())

from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify
from app.database import db
from app.models import LeagueTable, LeagueStanding
from sqlalchemy import desc

bp = Blueprint('tables', __name__)

@bp.route('/', methods=['GET'])
def get_league_table():
    season = request.args.get('season')
    if not season:
        abort(400, "season required")
    # try league_standings first
    standings = LeagueStanding.query.filter_by(season=season).order_by(LeagueStanding.points.desc()).all()
    if standings:
        return jsonify([s.to_dict() for s in standings])
    # fallback to league_tables
    table = LeagueTable.query.filter_by(season=season).order_by(LeagueTable.points.desc()).all()
    return jsonify([t.to_dict() for t in table])

@bp.route('/seasons', methods=['GET'])
def get_seasons():
    seasons = db.session.query(LeagueStanding.season).distinct().order_by(LeagueStanding.season.desc()).all()
    seasons_list = [s[0] for s in seasons if s[0]]
    if not seasons_list:
        seasons = db.session.query(LeagueTable.season).distinct().order_by(LeagueTable.season.desc()).all()
        seasons_list = [s[0] for s in seasons if s[0]]
    return jsonify(seasons_list)


====================================================================================================
FILE: app\routes\tables_old.py

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, joinedload
from typing import List
from datetime import datetime
from .. import database, models, schemas

router = APIRouter()

@router.get("/", response_model=List[schemas.LeagueTable])
async def get_league_table(
    season: str = Query(str(datetime.now().year), description="Season year"),
    db: Session = Depends(database.get_db)
):
    # Ensure we have team data loaded
    table = db.query(models.LeagueTable)\
        .filter(models.LeagueTable.season == season)\
        .options(joinedload(models.LeagueTable.team))\
        .order_by(
            models.LeagueTable.points.desc(),
            (models.LeagueTable.goals_for - models.LeagueTable.goals_against).desc(),
            models.LeagueTable.goals_for.desc()
        ).all()
    return table

@router.get("/seasons")
async def get_seasons(db: Session = Depends(database.get_db)):
    # Get distinct seasons from database, ordered descending
    seasons = db.query(models.LeagueTable.season).distinct().order_by(models.LeagueTable.season.desc()).all()
    seasons_list = [s[0] for s in seasons]
    current_year = str(datetime.now().year)
    # Always include current year at the top
    if current_year not in seasons_list:
        seasons_list.insert(0, current_year)
    return seasons_list

from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, abort
from flask_jwt_extended import jwt_required
from app.database import db
from app.models import User
from app.schemas import UserCreate
from app.auth import get_current_user, admin_required, get_password_hash

bp = Blueprint('users', __name__)

@bp.route('/', methods=['GET'])
@jwt_required()
def get_users():
    user = get_current_user()
    if user.role != 'admin':
        abort(403)
    skip = int(request.args.get('skip', 0))
    limit = int(request.args.get('limit', 100))
    users = User.query.offset(skip).limit(limit).all()
    return jsonify([u.to_dict() for u in users])

@bp.route('/', methods=['POST'])
@jwt_required()
def create_user():
    user = get_current_user()
    if user.role != 'admin':
        abort(403)
    data = request.get_json()
    existing = User.query.filter((User.username == data['username']) | (User.email == data['email'])).first()
    if existing:
        abort(400, "Username or email already exists")
    hashed = get_password_hash(data['password'])
    new_user = User(
        username=data['username'],
        email=data['email'],
        hashed_password=hashed,
        full_name=data['full_name'],
        role=data.get('role', 'viewer'),
        club_id=data.get('club_id')
    )
    db.session.add(new_user)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({"id": str(new_user.id)}), 201

@bp.route('/me', methods=['GET'])
@jwt_required()
def get_current_user_info():
    user = get_current_user()
    return jsonify(user.to_dict())

# app/schemas/_base.py
from pydantic import BaseModel, EmailStr, Field
from datetime import datetime, date
from typing import Optional, List
import uuid
from uuid import UUID
import enum

# ---------- Enums (for schemas) ----------
class UserRoleEnum(str, enum.Enum):
    ADMIN = "admin"
    MATCH_COMMISSIONER = "match_commissioner"
    MEDIA_OFFICER = "media_officer"
    CLUB_REP = "club_rep"
    REFEREE = "referee"
    VIEWER = "viewer"

class MatchEventType(str, enum.Enum):
    GOAL = "goal"
    ASSIST = "assist"
    YELLOW_CARD = "yellow_card"
    RED_CARD = "red_card"
    SUBSTITUTION = "substitution"
    PENALTY = "penalty"
    OWN_GOAL = "own_goal"

class Gender(str, enum.Enum):
    MALE = "male"
    FEMALE = "female"

class LeagueLevel(str, enum.Enum):
    SUPER_LEAGUE = "super_league"
    DIVISION_ONE = "division_one"
    DIVISION_TWO = "division_two"
    DIVISION_THREE = "division_three"
    PROVINCIAL = "provincial"
    NATIONAL_LEAGUE_SOUTH = "national_league_south"
    NATIONAL_LEAGUE_NORTH = "national_league_north"

class MatchType(str, enum.Enum):
    LEAGUE = "league"
    CUP = "cup"
    FRIENDLY = "friendly"
    TOURNAMENT = "tournament"
    QUALIFIER = "qualifier"

class TournamentCategory(str, enum.Enum):
    FRIENDLY = "friendly"
    COSAFA = "cosafa"
    AFCON = "afcon"
    WAFCON = "wafcon"
    FOUR_NATIONS = "four_nations"
    CHAN = "chan"
    FIFA_WORLD_CUP = "fifa_world_cup"
    OLYMPICS = "olympics"

====================================================================================================
FILE: app\ticketing\admin.py
====================================================================================================

# app/ticketing/admin.py
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify
from flask_jwt_extended import jwt_required, get_jwt_identity
from app.database import db
from app.models import Club, TicketMatch, TicketType, User, Order
from app.auth import get_current_user, get_password_hash

bp = Blueprint('ticketing_admin', __name__, url_prefix='/api/ticketing/admin')

def admin_required(f):
    from functools import wraps
    @wraps(f)
    @jwt_required()
    def decorated(*args, **kwargs):
        user_id = get_jwt_identity()
        user = User.query.get(user_id)
        if not user or (user.role not in ['admin', 'super_admin']):
            return jsonify({'error': 'Admin access required'}), 403
        return f(*args, **kwargs)
    return decorated

# ---------- Clubs ----------
@bp.route('/clubs', methods=['POST'])
@admin_required
def create_club():
    data = request.get_json()
    club = Club(
        name=data['name'],
        short_name=data.get('short_name'),
        logo_url=data.get('logo_url'),
        description=data.get('description')
    )
    db.session.add(club)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify(club.to_dict()), 201

@bp.route('/clubs/<club_id>', methods=['PUT'])
@admin_required
def update_club(club_id):
    club = Club.query.get_or_404(club_id)
    data = request.get_json()
    for field in ['name', 'short_name', 'logo_url', 'description', 'is_active']:
        if field in data:
            setattr(club, field, data[field])
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify(club.to_dict()), 200

@bp.route('/clubs/<club_id>', methods=['DELETE'])
@admin_required
def delete_club(club_id):
    club = Club.query.get_or_404(club_id)
    club.is_active = False
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({'message': 'Club deactivated'}), 200

# ---------- Ticket Matches ----------
@bp.route('/matches', methods=['POST'])
@admin_required
def create_match():
    data = request.get_json()
    user_id = get_jwt_identity()
    match = TicketMatch(
        home_team=data['home_team'],
        away_team=data['away_team'],
        match_type=data['match_type'],
        club_id=data.get('club_id'),
        venue=data['venue'],
        date_time=data['date_time'],
        description=data.get('description'),
        created_by=user_id
    )
    db.session.add(match)
    db.session.flush()

    for tt_data in data.get('ticket_types', []):
        tt = TicketType(
            ticket_match_id=match.id,
            name=tt_data['name'],
            price=tt_data['price'],
            capacity=tt_data['capacity']
        )
        db.session.add(tt)

    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify(match.to_dict()), 201

@bp.route('/matches/<match_id>', methods=['PUT'])
@admin_required
def update_match(match_id):
    match = TicketMatch.query.get_or_404(match_id)
    data = request.get_json()

    updatable = ['home_team', 'away_team', 'venue', 'date_time', 'description', 'is_active']
    for field in updatable:
        if field in data:
            setattr(match, field, data[field])

    if 'ticket_types' in data:
        incoming = []
        for tt_data in data['ticket_types']:
            name = tt_data.get('name', '').strip()
            price = tt_data.get('price')
            capacity = tt_data.get('capacity')
            if name and price is not None and capacity is not None:
                incoming.append({
                    'id': tt_data.get('id'),
                    'name': name,
                    'price': float(price),
                    'capacity': int(capacity)
                })

        existing_map = {tt.id: tt for tt in match.ticket_types}
        kept_ids = set()

        for item in incoming:
            if item['id'] and item['id'] in existing_map:
                tt = existing_map[item['id']]
                tt.name = item['name']
                tt.price = item['price']
                tt.capacity = item['capacity']
                kept_ids.add(item['id'])
            else:
                new_tt = TicketType(
                    ticket_match_id=match.id,
                    name=item['name'],
                    price=item['price'],
                    capacity=item['capacity']
                )
                db.session.add(new_tt)
                db.session.flush()
                kept_ids.add(new_tt.id)

        for tt in match.ticket_types:
            if tt.id not in kept_ids:
                db.session.delete(tt)

    db.session.commit()
    db.session.refresh(match)
    return jsonify(match.to_dict()), 200

@bp.route('/matches/<match_id>', methods=['DELETE'])
@admin_required
def delete_match(match_id):
    match = TicketMatch.query.get_or_404(match_id)
    db.session.delete(match)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({'message': 'Match deleted'}), 200

        role=data.get('role', 'viewer'),
        club_id=data.get('club_id')

        user.email = data['email']

        user.full_name = data['full_name']
    if 'role' in data:

    if 'club_id' in data:
        user.club_id = data['club_id']
    if 'is_active' in data:
        user.is_active = data['is_active']
    if 'password' in data and data['password']:
        user.hashed_password = get_password_hash(data['password'])
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify(user.to_dict()), 200

# app/ticketing/api.py
from app.models.ticket import Ticket
from flask import Blueprint, jsonify, request
from flask_jwt_extended import decode_token
from app.database import db
from app.models import TicketMatch, Club, TicketType, Order, OrderItem, SeatHold, User
from app.auth import get_current_user_optional
import datetime

bp = Blueprint('ticketing_api', __name__, url_prefix='/api/ticketing')

# ---------- Clubs (public) ----------
@bp.route('/clubs', methods=['GET'])
def get_clubs():
    active_only = request.args.get('active_only', 'true').lower() == 'true'
    query = Club.query
    if active_only:
        query = query.filter_by(is_active=True)
    clubs = query.all()
    return jsonify([c.to_dict() for c in clubs]), 200

@bp.route('/clubs/<club_id>', methods=['GET'])
def get_club(club_id):
    club = Club.query.get_or_404(club_id)
    return jsonify(club.to_dict()), 200

# ---------- Matches (public) ----------
@bp.route('/matches', methods=['GET'])
def get_matches():
    match_type = request.args.get('type')
    club_id = request.args.get('club_id')
    active_only = request.args.get('active_only', 'true').lower() == 'true'
    query = TicketMatch.query
    if match_type:
        query = query.filter_by(match_type=match_type)
    if club_id:
        query = query.filter_by(club_id=club_id)
    if active_only:
        query = query.filter_by(is_active=True)
    matches = query.order_by(TicketMatch.date_time.asc()).all()
    return jsonify([m.to_dict() for m in matches]), 200

@bp.route('/matches/<match_id>', methods=['GET'])
def get_match(match_id):
    clean_expired_holds()
    match = TicketMatch.query.get_or_404(match_id)
    return jsonify(match.to_dict()), 200

@bp.route('/matches/<match_id>/ticket-types', methods=['GET'])
def get_match_ticket_types(match_id):
    match = TicketMatch.query.get_or_404(match_id)
    ticket_types = TicketType.query.filter_by(ticket_match_id=match_id).all()
    return jsonify([tt.to_dict() for tt in ticket_types]), 200

# ---------- Holds (public) ----------
def clean_expired_holds():
    SeatHold.query.filter(SeatHold.expires_at < datetime.utcnow()).delete()
    db.session.commit()

@bp.route('/matches/<match_id>/hold', methods=['POST'])
def hold_tickets(match_id):
    data = request.get_json()
    if not data:
        return jsonify({'error': 'No data provided'}), 400

    ticket_type_id = data.get('ticket_type_id')
    try:
        quantity = int(data.get('quantity', 0))
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return jsonify({'error': 'Invalid quantity'}), 400

    hold_token = data.get('hold_token')

    clean_expired_holds()

    ticket_type = TicketType.query.get(ticket_type_id)
    if not ticket_type or ticket_type.ticket_match_id != match_id:
        return jsonify({'error': 'Ticket type not found'}), 404

    current_hold = None
    if hold_token:
        current_hold = SeatHold.query.filter_by(hold_token=hold_token).first()
        if not current_hold:
            return jsonify({'error': 'Hold token not found'}), 404

    held_by_others = db.session.query(db.func.sum(SeatHold.quantity)).filter(
        SeatHold.ticket_type_id == ticket_type_id,
        SeatHold.id != (current_hold.id if current_hold else 0)
    ).scalar() or 0

    available = ticket_type.capacity - ticket_type.tickets_sold - held_by_others
    if quantity > available:
        return jsonify({'error': f'Only {available} seats available', 'available': available}), 409

    if current_hold:
        current_hold.quantity = quantity
        current_hold.expires_at = datetime.utcnow() + timedelta(minutes=10)
        db.session.commit()
        return jsonify({
            'hold_token': current_hold.hold_token,
            'quantity': quantity,
            'available': available - quantity
        }), 200
    else:
        if quantity == 0:
            return jsonify({'error': 'Quantity must be positive'}), 400
        new_hold = SeatHold(
            ticket_match_id=match_id,
            ticket_type_id=ticket_type_id,
            quantity=quantity
        )
        db.session.add(new_hold)
        db.session.commit()
        return jsonify({
            'hold_token': new_hold.hold_token,
            'quantity': quantity,
            'available': available - quantity
        }), 201

@bp.route('/matches/<match_id>/hold/<hold_token>', methods=['DELETE'])
def release_hold(match_id, hold_token):
    hold = SeatHold.query.filter_by(hold_token=hold_token, ticket_match_id=match_id).first()
    if not hold:
        return jsonify({'error': 'Hold not found'}), 404
    db.session.delete(hold)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({'message': 'Hold released'}), 200

# ---------- Orders (public) ----------
@bp.route('/orders', methods=['POST'])
def create_order():
    data = request.get_json()
    if not data:
        return jsonify({'error': 'No data provided'}), 400

    # Optional user from token
    user_id = None
    auth_header = request.headers.get('Authorization')
    if auth_header and auth_header.startswith('Bearer '):
        try:
            token = auth_header.split(' ')[1]
            decoded = decode_token(token)
            user_id = decoded['sub']
        except:
            pass

    customer_email = data.get('customer_email')
    customer_name = data.get('customer_name')
    customer_phone = data.get('customer_phone')
    items = data.get('items', [])
    hold_tokens = data.get('hold_tokens', [])

    if not customer_email or not items:
        return jsonify({'error': 'Email and items required'}), 400

    with db.session.begin_nested():
        total = 0
        order_items = []
        for item in items:
            ticket_type_id = item.get('ticket_type_id')
            quantity = item.get('quantity')
            if not ticket_type_id or not quantity or quantity <= 0:
                db.session.rollback()
                return jsonify({'error': 'Invalid item data'}), 400

            ticket_type = db.session.query(TicketType).filter(
                TicketType.id == ticket_type_id
            ).with_for_update().one_or_none()
            if not ticket_type:
                db.session.rollback()
                return jsonify({'error': f'Ticket type {ticket_type_id} not found'}), 404

            available = ticket_type.capacity - ticket_type.tickets_sold
            if quantity > available:
                db.session.rollback()
                return jsonify({'error': f'Only {available} tickets available for {ticket_type.name}'}), 400

            total += float(ticket_type.price) * quantity
            order_items.append({
                'ticket_type': ticket_type,
                'quantity': quantity,
                'price': float(ticket_type.price)
            })

            ticket_type.tickets_sold += quantity
            db.session.add(ticket_type)

        order = Order(
            user_id=user_id,
            customer_email=customer_email,
            customer_name=customer_name,
            customer_phone=customer_phone,
            total_amount=total,
            payment_status='pending',
            payment_method='demo'
        )
        db.session.add(order)
        db.session.flush()

        for item in order_items:
            order_item = OrderItem(
                order_id=order.id,
                ticket_type_id=item['ticket_type'].id,
                quantity=item['quantity'],
                unit_price=item['ticket_type'].price
            )
            db.session.add(order_item)

        for token in hold_tokens:
            hold = SeatHold.query.filter_by(hold_token=token).first()
            if hold:
                db.session.delete(hold)

    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify(order.to_dict()), 201

@bp.route('/orders/<order_id>/cancel', methods=['POST'])
def cancel_order(order_id):
    order = Order.query.get_or_404(order_id)
    if order.payment_status != 'pending':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Only pending orders can be cancelled'}), 400

    for item in order.items:
        ticket_type = TicketType.query.get(item.ticket_type_id)
        if ticket_type:
            ticket_type.tickets_sold = max(0, ticket_type.tickets_sold - item.quantity)
            db.session.add(ticket_type)

    db.session.delete(order)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({'message': 'Order cancelled'}), 200

@bp.route('/orders/by-number/<order_number>', methods=['GET'])
def get_order_by_number(order_number):
    order = Order.query.filter_by(order_number=order_number).first_or_404()
    return jsonify(order.to_dict()), 200

@bp.route('/orders/<order_id>', methods=['GET'])
def get_order(order_id):
    order = Order.query.get_or_404(order_id)
    return jsonify(order.to_dict()), 200

# ---------- Tickets (public) ----------
@bp.route('/tickets/verify', methods=['POST'])
def verify_ticket():
    data = request.get_json()
    ticket_number = data.get('ticket_number')
    if not ticket_number:
        return jsonify({'valid': False, 'message': 'Ticket number required'}), 400

    ticket = Ticket.query.filter_by(ticket_number=ticket_number).first()
    if not ticket:
        return jsonify({'valid': False, 'message': 'Ticket not found'}), 404

    if ticket.is_used:
        return jsonify({'valid': False, 'message': 'Ticket already used'}), 400

    # Mark as used
    ticket.is_used = True
    ticket.used_at = db.func.now()
    # verified_by can be set if user logged in
    db.session.commit()

    return jsonify({
        'valid': True,
        'ticket': ticket.to_dict(),
        'match': ticket.ticket_match.to_dict() if ticket.ticket_match else None
    }), 200

@bp.route('/tickets/<ticket_number>', methods=['GET'])
def get_ticket(ticket_number):
    ticket = Ticket.query.filter_by(ticket_number=ticket_number).first_or_404()
    return jsonify(ticket.to_dict()), 200

    from app.models import CompetitionMatch
    matches = CompetitionMatch.query.filter_by(is_ticketed=True, status='scheduled').order_by(CompetitionMatch.match_date).all()
    return jsonify([m.to_dict() for m in matches]), 200

# app/ticketing/auth.py
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify
from flask_jwt_extended import create_access_token, jwt_required, get_jwt_identity
from app.database import db
from app.models import User
from app.auth import get_password_hash, verify_password
from datetime import timedelta

bp = Blueprint('ticketing_auth', __name__, url_prefix='/auth')

@bp.route('/ping', methods=['GET'])
def ping():
    return jsonify({'message': 'Auth blueprint is alive!'}), 200

@bp.route('/register', methods=['POST'])
def register():
    data = request.get_json()
    if not data:
        return jsonify({'error': 'No data provided'}), 400

    email = data.get('email')
    password = data.get('password')
    first_name = data.get('first_name')
    last_name = data.get('last_name')
    phone = data.get('phone')
    role = data.get('role', 'viewer')

    if not email or not password:
        return jsonify({'error': 'Email and password required'}), 400

    if User.query.filter_by(email=email).first():
        return jsonify({'error': 'Email already registered'}), 409

    user = User(
        email=email,
        username=email,
        full_name=f"{first_name or ''} {last_name or ''}".strip(),
        role=role,
        is_active=True
    )
    user.hashed_password = get_password_hash(password)

    db.session.add(user)
    db.session.commit()

    access_token = create_access_token(
        identity=str(user.id),
        additional_claims={'role': user.role},
        expires_delta=timedelta(hours=24)
    )
    return jsonify({
        'message': 'Registration successful',
        'user': user.to_dict(),
        'access_token': access_token
    }), 201

@bp.route('/login', methods=['POST'])
def login():
    data = request.get_json()
    if not data:
        return jsonify({'error': 'No data provided'}), 400

    email = data.get('email')
    password = data.get('password')

    if not email or not password:
        return jsonify({'error': 'Email and password required'}), 400

    user = User.query.filter_by(email=email).first()
    if not user:
        return jsonify({'error': 'User not found'}), 401

    if not verify_password(password, user.hashed_password):
        return jsonify({'error': 'Invalid password'}), 401

    if not user.is_active:
        return jsonify({'error': 'Account disabled'}), 403

    access_token = create_access_token(
        identity=str(user.id),
        additional_claims={'role': user.role},
        expires_delta=timedelta(hours=24)
    )
    return jsonify({
        'user': user.to_dict(),
        'access_token': access_token
    }), 200

@bp.route('/me', methods=['GET'])
@jwt_required()
def get_current_user():
    user_id = get_jwt_identity()
    user = User.query.get(user_id)
    if not user:
        return jsonify({'error': 'User not found'}), 404
    return jsonify(user.to_dict()), 200

# app/ticketing/club.py
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify
from flask_jwt_extended import jwt_required, get_jwt_identity
from app.database import db
from app.models import User, Club, TicketMatch, TicketType
from app.auth import get_current_user

bp = Blueprint('ticketing_club', __name__, url_prefix='/api/ticketing/club')

def club_access_required(f):
    from functools import wraps
    @wraps(f)
    @jwt_required()
    def decorated(*args, **kwargs):
        user_id = get_jwt_identity()
        user = User.query.get(user_id)
        if not user:
            return jsonify({'error': 'User not found'}), 401

        # Admins and users with club roles allowed
        if user.role in ['admin', 'club_admin', 'club_officer']:
            return f(*args, **kwargs)
        return jsonify({'error': 'Access denied'}), 403
    return decorated

@bp.route('/dashboard', methods=['GET'])
@club_access_required
def dashboard():
    user_id = get_jwt_identity()
    user = User.query.get(user_id)

    if user.role in ['admin', 'super_admin']:
        # For admins, we can show all clubs or first active
        club = Club.query.filter_by(is_active=True).first()
    else:
        club = Club.query.get(user.club_id)
        if not club:
            return jsonify({
                'club': None,
                'upcoming_matches': [],
                'total_tickets_sold': 0,
                'total_revenue': 0,
                'needs_club_assignment': True,
                'message': 'Your account is not yet linked to a club. Please contact an administrator.'
            }), 200

    if not club:
        return jsonify({
            'club': None,
            'upcoming_matches': [],
            'total_tickets_sold': 0,
            'total_revenue': 0,
            'needs_club_setup': True,
            'message': 'No active clubs found. Please create a club first.'
        }), 200

    matches = TicketMatch.query.filter_by(club_id=club.id, match_type='club').order_by(TicketMatch.date_time.desc()).limit(5).all()
    return jsonify({
        'club': club.to_dict(),
        'upcoming_matches': [m.to_dict() for m in matches],
        'total_tickets_sold': 0,
        'total_revenue': 0
    }), 200

@bp.route('/matches', methods=['POST'])
@club_access_required
def create_match():
    user_id = get_jwt_identity()
    user = User.query.get(user_id)
    data = request.get_json()

    if user.role in ['admin', 'super_admin']:
        club_id = data.get('club_id')
        if not club_id:
            club = Club.query.filter_by(is_active=True).first()
            if not club:
                return jsonify({'error': 'No active clubs found. Please create a club first.'}), 400
            club_id = club.id
    else:
        club_id = user.club_id
        if not club_id:
            return jsonify({'error': 'Your account is not linked to a club.'}), 400

    match = TicketMatch(
        home_team=data['home_team'],
        away_team=data['away_team'],
        match_type='club',
        club_id=club_id,
        venue=data['venue'],
        date_time=data['date_time'],
        description=data.get('description'),
        created_by=user_id
    )
    db.session.add(match)
    db.session.flush()

    for tt_data in data.get('ticket_types', []):
        tt = TicketType(
            ticket_match_id=match.id,
            name=tt_data['name'],
            price=tt_data['price'],
            capacity=tt_data['capacity']
        )
        db.session.add(tt)

    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify(match.to_dict()), 201

@bp.route('/matches', methods=['GET'])
@club_access_required
def get_club_matches():
    user_id = get_jwt_identity()
    user = User.query.get(user_id)

    if user.role in ['admin', 'super_admin']:
        club_id_param = request.args.get('club_id')
        if club_id_param:
            matches = TicketMatch.query.filter_by(club_id=club_id_param, match_type='club').order_by(TicketMatch.date_time.desc()).all()
        else:
            matches = TicketMatch.query.filter_by(match_type='club').order_by(TicketMatch.date_time.desc()).all()
    else:
        matches = TicketMatch.query.filter_by(club_id=user.club_id, match_type='club').order_by(TicketMatch.date_time.desc()).all()

    return jsonify([m.to_dict() for m in matches]), 200

@bp.route('/matches/<match_id>', methods=['PUT'])
@club_access_required
def update_match(match_id):
    user_id = get_jwt_identity()
    user = User.query.get(user_id)
    match = TicketMatch.query.get_or_404(match_id)

    if not (user.role in ['admin', 'super_admin']):
        if match.club_id != user.club_id:
            return jsonify({'error': 'Unauthorized'}), 403

    data = request.get_json()
    updatable = ['home_team', 'away_team', 'venue', 'date_time', 'description', 'is_active']
    for field in updatable:
        if field in data:
            setattr(match, field, data[field])
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify(match.to_dict()), 200
